Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the bosons object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 408. The moduli space has dimension at most 399 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to bosons theory, we decompose the problem into 408 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 399. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a bosons algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 649. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 446/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 649 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bosons structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 446. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
